Active-set Newton:
- Given a, choose active set P indices where c > M - tolerance (or top r). Target T = mean c_P. Solve for δ in least squares: (a*δ)_k = (T - c_k)/2 for k∈P, with sum δ=0, and norm bound. This directly flattens active peaks.
- We can also use Moore-Penrose with constraint sum zero by adding row of ones with small weight to least squares. - Use active set top r (e.g. min(n, number above threshold)). If r<n, delta degrees freedom enough. Add ridge `lambda I` to avoid ill-conditioned. We can solve via normal equations with SVD? Use `np.linalg.lstsq`.

Let's design low-dim optimization concretely. Use features F (n, d). We want optimize b. The value for L-BFGS: Maybe use logsumexp with gamma set fixed per run (e.g. gamma = 20*n). Gradient: as above. The low-dim objective function top-level:
```
def _lowdim_value(b, F, gamma):
    x = F @ b
    a = _softmax(x)
    c = _convolve_fft(a)
    u = gamma * c
    u = u - u.max()
    return float(u.max() + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(u)))) # since subtract max
```
But value should be gamma*max + logsumexp shifted = gamma*max + logsumexp; if subtract max: `maxc + logsumexp`. Since gamma*maxc? Wait u=gamma c; shift by maxu=gamma maxc; F= maxu + logsumexp(exp(u-maxu)). So return maxu + logsumexp. Good.
```
def _lowdim_grad(b, F, gamma):
   x=F@b; a=softmax; c=conv
   u=gamma*c; shift; w=exp(u-shift)/sum
   val=shift+logsum
   g_a = 2*gamma * corr(w,a)
   gx = F.T @ a*(g_a - dot(a,g_a))
   return val, gx
```

x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)

e = np.exp(x)

if s <=0 or not finite: return np.full_like(x,1/len(x))

```
- `_convolve_raw(a)`:

# due to roundoff negative
return np.maximum(c, 0.0)
```

def _soft_adam(...):
  n=len(a0); a=np.clip...; a=a/a.sum() if sum>0 else ones/n
  x=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12)); x-=x.mean()
  m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
  best_a=a.copy(); best_score=2*n*_convolve_raw(a).max()
  no_improve=0
  start_best=best_score
  while t<max_iters and time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
    a=_softmax(x)
    c=_convolve_raw(a); M=max
    score=2*n*M
    if score<best_score:
      best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
    else: no_improve+=1
    if no_improve>3000:
       # random injection
       x += noise_scale * rng.normal(size=n)
       m*=0.5; v*=0.5; no_improve=0
    frac=t/max_iters
    p = p_start + (p_end-p_start)*(frac**2)
    w=_lp_weights(c,p)
    ga=2.0*_grad_corr(w,a)  # scalar not important
    gx=a*(ga - np.dot(a,ga))
    t+=1
    m=0.9*m+0.1*gx
    v=0.999*v+0.001*gx*gx
    x_lr=lr/(np.sqrt(v)+1e-8) # but m bias? use simple 0.1?
    x = x - x_lr*np.sign? Use Adam:
       m_hat=m/(1-0.9**t)
       vx_hat=...; x=x-lr*m_hat/(sqrt(vx_hat)+eps)
    # optional clip x to [-40,40]
    x = np.clip(x, -40, 40)
  return best_a,best_score
```

- `_newton_flatten(a0, deadline, max_steps, active_frac=1.0)`:
Let's implement robust.
```
def _newton_flatten(a, deadline, max_steps=40, active_frac=1.0, ridge=1e-6):
   n=len(a); a=a.copy(); a/=a.sum(); best=a.copy(); best_score=2n*maxc
   # choose r list
   r_list=[]
   for frac in [0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0]: r=max(8,min(n,int(n*frac)))...
   for r in r_list:
      step_fails=0
      for st in range(max_steps):
        if time > deadline-2 or step_fails>5: break
        c=conv(a); M=max; score=...
        update best
        if M <= best_score/(2n) + 1e-15: break
        active_idx=np.argsort(c)[-r:]
        active_c=c[active_idx]
        target=float(np.mean(active_c))
        # If target not less than max, maybe target = max*0.95? mean might equal if all equal; skip
        if target >= active_c.max()*(1-1e-12): break
        K=np.zeros((r,n))
        # build rows with vectorized loop
        for row, m in enumerate(active_idx):
           lo=max(0, m-n+1); hi=min(n-1, m)
           if hi>=lo:
              idx=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
              K[row,idx]=a[m-idx]
        # weights emphasize high: active_max -? Use w = active_c ** 8 maybe
        # scale rows: sqrtw
        aw = active_c ** 6  # or exp
        aw = aw / max(1e-30, aw.max())
        sqrtw=np.sqrt(aw)
        rhs = 0.5*(target - active_c) * sqrtw
        Ks = K * sqrtw[:,None]
        # add ridge to avoid NaN: use (Ks.T@Ks + ridge I) solve Ks.T rhs
        # Use lstsq
        try:
           delta, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(Ks, rhs, rcond=None)
        except: break
        # optional linear sum constraint: delta -= mean
        delta -= delta.mean()
        # scale step
        dn=np.linalg.norm(delta); if dn<1e-12: break
        delta = delta * (min(1.0, 0.5*?) / dn)
        accepted=False
        for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
          for eps in (1.0,0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05):
             a_new=a + sign*eps*delta
             if a_new.min() < 0:
                # if small negative? clip; else skip if large
                if a_new.min() > -1e-6: a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0.0)
                else: continue
             s=a_new.sum()
             if s<=1e-12: continue
             a_new = a_new / s
             cnew=conv(a_new); Mnew=float(cnew.max()); scorenew=2*n*Mnew
             if scorenew < best_score*(1-1e-12):
                 a=a_new; best=a_new.copy(); best_score=scorenew; accepted=True; step_fails=0; break
          if accepted: break
        if not accepted: step_fails +=1
   return best,best_score
```

def _full_obj(x, gamma):
    a=_softmax(x)
    c=_convolve_fft(a)
    u=gamma*c
    shift=u.max()
    z=np.sum(np.exp(u-shift))
    return float(shift + np.log(z))
```

Gradient:
```
def _full_jac(x, gamma):
    a=_softmax(x); c=...; u=gamma*c; shift; e=exp(u-shift); w=e/e.sum()
    ga=2.0*gamma*_grad_corr(w,a)
    g= a*(ga - np.dot(a, ga))
    return g
```

```
def _feature_matrix(n, kind):
    t = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    cols=[]
    cols.append(np.ones(n))
    for p in range(1,9): cols.append(t**p)
    if kind==0:
        pass
    elif kind==1:
        for m in range(1,4):
            cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*m*t))
            cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*m*t))
    else:
        cols.append(np.tanh(4.0*(t-0.5)))
        cols.append(np.tanh(8.0*(t-0.5)))
        cols.append(np.tanh(12.0*(t-0.5)))
        cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*t + np.cos(2*np.pi*t)))? maybe.
    F=np.column_stack(cols)
    return F
```

Let's write `_refine_candidate`:
```
def _refine_candidate(a0, deadline, budget, seed=0):
    n=len(a0); a=np.asarray(a0,float).copy(); S=a.sum(); if S<=0: return None, inf
    a=np.clip(a,0,1e9); a/=S
    best=a; best_score=2*n*_convolve_raw(a).max()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    remaining = budget
    # We can take phases until now > deadline
    # Phase 1: soft low
    stop = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + min(remaining*0.25, 90))
    if time < stop:
       a1,s1=_soft_adam(a, stop, max_iters=100000, p_start=2,p_end=32,lr=0.05,seed=seed+1)
       if s1<best_score: best=a1; best_score=s1
    if time<deadline-1:
       # Polish L-BFGS if enough time left: need at least 30s
       remaining_to_deadline = deadline - time.monotonic()
       alloc = min(60, remaining_to_deadline*0.2)
       if alloc>25:
          gamma = 10.0*n
          a2,s2=_lbfgs_full(best, gamma, time.monotonic()+alloc, maxiter=200)
          update
    if time<deadline-1 and n<=512:
       alloc = min(45, (deadline-now)*0.25)
       if alloc>30:
          a3,s3=_newton_flatten(best, time.monotonic()+alloc, max_steps=25)
    # Phase soft high
    stop=min(deadline, now+min(remaining*0.25,90))
    a4,s4=_soft_adam(best, stop, max_iters=200000, p_start=16,p_end=128, lr=0.02, seed=seed+2)
    # If n<=512 final Newton
    # Random perturbation soft maybe
```
But this may not use full budget if alloc small. Instead use `_refine_candidate` with an absolute stop at time+budget; phases just partition. Potential sign of gradient in Adam: We want minimize Lp. We computed `ga=corr(w,a)`. Gradient of F wrt a positive for parameters that increase high c. To minimize, subtract. `gx=a*(ga - dot)`. def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

def _newton_flatten(a0, deadline, max_steps=25):
   a=_... normalize
   best=a; best_score=score
   r_list=[]
   if n<=600:
      for frac in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5): r=int(...)
      unique sorted
   else: r_list=[32,64,128]
   # For each r, run some steps until no improvements
   for r in r_list:
      fails=0
      for st in range(max_steps):
         if time > deadline-1: break
         c=conv(a); current= score
         update best
         if active count <=1: break
         idx=np.argsort(c)[-r:] # ascending
         idx=idx[::-1]
         active_c=c[idx]
         target=mean(active_c)
         if target >= active_c[0]*(1-1e-10): break
         K=...
         w_pos=0.7**np.arange(r)
         rhs = 0.5*(target - active_c)*np.sqrt(w_pos)
         Ks = K*np.sqrt(w_pos)[:,None]
         try delta=lstsq(Ks, rhs, rcond=1e-10)[0]
         except: break
         delta-=delta.mean()
         if not all finite: break
         dn=norm(delta); if dn<=0: break
         cap = max(1e-6, 0.5*float(np.linalg.norm(a)))? We see raw delta maybe. Let's cap to `cap = max(1e-6, 2.0*float(np.linalg.norm(a)))`. norm(a)=~1/sqrt(n). For n=512 cap .088; raw .04. okay. If dn>cap: delta*=cap/dn
         accepted=False
         for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
            for eps in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01):
               a_new = a + sign*eps*delta
               a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
               S=a_new.sum()
               if S <=1e-12: continue
               a_new /= S
               cnew=conv(a_new); M=float(cnew.max()); scorenew=2*n*M
               if scorenew < best_score - 1e-12:
                  a=a_new; best=a_new.copy(); best_score=scorenew; accepted=True; fails=0; break
            if accepted: break
         if not accepted:
            fails +=1
            if fails>6: break
      if time>deadline-1: break
   return best,best_score
```

def _refine_candidate(a0, deadline, budget, seed=0):
   if budget <=0: return None, inf
   a=np.maximum(np.asarray(a0,float),0); S=a.sum(); if S<=0: return None, inf
   a=a/S
   best=a; best_score=2*len(a)*_convolve_raw(a).max()
   start=time.monotonic()
   stop_all=min(deadline, start+float(budget))
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   n=len(a)
   # If budget small or time small: soft only
   if stop_all - time.monotonic() > 10:
      # soft low
      stop1 = min(stop_all, start + 0.25*budget)
      if stop1 - time.monotonic() > 5:
         a1,s1=_soft_adam(best, stop1, max_iters=100000, p_start=2, p_end=32, lr=0.06, seed=seed+1)
         if s1<best_score: best=a1; best_score=s1
   if n <= 4096 and stop_all - time.monotonic() > 30:
      # lbfgs low
      alloc = min(50.0, (stop_all - time.monotonic()) * 0.3)
      if time.monotonic() + alloc < stop_all - 5:
         gamma=10.0*n
         a2,s2=_lbfgs_full(best, gamma, time.monotonic()+alloc, maxiter=150)
         update
   if n <= 700 and stop_all - time.monotonic() > 60:
      alloc = min(60.0, (stop_all - time.monotonic()) * 0.3)
      if ...
         a3,s3=_newton_flatten(best, time.monotonic()+alloc, max_steps=30)
   # soft high
   stop4 = min(stop_all, start + 0.75*budget)
   if time.monotonic()+5 < stop4:
       a4,s4=_soft_adam(best, stop4, max_iters=200000, p_start=8, p_end=128, lr=0.025, seed=seed+2)
   # final lbfgs high

   # soft final if time left

   # ensure best valid, return
```

```
def _lbfgs_full(a0, gamma, deadline, maxiter=200):
   a=np.asarray... normalize
   best=a; best_score=score
   x=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12)); x-=np.mean(x)
   global _LBFSGS_DEADLINE
   _LBFSGS_DEADLINE = deadline - 2.0
   try:
      res=minimize(_full_obj, x, args=(gamma,), jac=_full_jac, method='L-BFGS-B', callback=_lbfgs_callback, options=...)
      if res.x is not None and finite:
         a2=_softmax(res.x); sc=...
         if sc<best_score: best=a2; best_score=sc
   except Exception: pass
   return best,best_score
```

          a2,s2=_refine_candidate(best_seq, deadline, alloc, seed=seed+1000)
          if s2<best_score: update
       # one more short final soft/newton? _refine does.
    # final fallback
    if best_seq is None:
       best_seq=np.ones(256); best_score=2*256*_convolve_raw(best_seq/256?). Hmm.
    best_seq=np.clip(np.asarray(best_seq,float),0,1000)
    best_seq/=best_seq.sum()
    seq_list=[float(x) for x in best_seq]
    try: ev=float(evaluate_sequence(seq_list)); print; if not finite fallback
    except: pass
    return seq_list
```

def _convolve_fft_and_grad(a, w):
   n=len(a); L=2n-1; N=...
   A=np.fft.fft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.ifft(A*A,N).real[:L]
   P=np.fft.fft(w,N)
   g=np.fft.ifft(P*np.conj(A),N).real[:n]
   return max(c,0), g
```

Implement `_lp_weights`:
```
def _lp_weights(c, p):
   L=len(c)
   if L==0: return np.array([])
   if p <= 1.0:
      return np.full(L, 1.0/L)
   cmax=c.max()
   if cmax <= 0:
      return np.full(L, 1.0/L)
   cc=np.maximum(c, 1e-300)
   logw=(p-1.0)*np.log(cc)
   logw=logw - logw.max()
   w=np.exp(logw)
   sw=w.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <=0:
      return np.full(L,1.0/L)
   w/=sw
   return w
```
For p large, logw may be all -inf? cmax positive, max log finite; at least one exp0. Good.

In Newton, weights `wpos` normalized? Not necessary but for lstsq scale. Use `wpow = 0.7 ** np.arange(r)`; `wpow = wpow / wpow.sum()` maybe; sqrt.

```python
"""
Optimizer for non-negative sequences a of length n minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

Strategy:
- Work on the probability simplex (sum(a)=1), where the objective is 2*n*max(conv).
- Use several diverse starts: previous best if available, low-dimensional L-BFGS
  on polynomial/sinusoidal log-linear models, random Dirichlet starts, block/ramp
  shapes, and flat-spectrum-inspired random starts.
- Refine candidates with:
    * smooth Lp / log-sum-exp gradient in the log-softmax parametrization,
      using FFT-accelerated autocorrelation gradients;
    * full-dimensional L-BFGS on the same log-sum-exp surrogate;
    * active-set Newton-style equalizing steps for the largest convolution terms,
      solved by weighted least squares and line-searched.
- Always validate against an internal FFT convolution and, when possible, call the
  external evaluate_sequence() once at the end.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_LBFSGS_DEADLINE = 0.0


def _next_pow2(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _softmax(x):
    """Stable softmax."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x.copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(x))
    e = np.exp(x - m)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(x.shape, 1.0 / float(x.size))
    return e / s


def _convolve_fft(a):
    """Linear convolution a * a via FFT, length 2n-1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(L)
    A = np.fft.fft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.ifft(A * A, N).real[:L]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def _corr_p_a(w, a):
    """
    g_i = sum_j a_j w_{i+j}, i=0..n-1.
    This is the convolution-gradient building block.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=float)
    n = len(a)
    L = len(w)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    N = _next_pow2(max(L, 2 * n - 1))
    P = np.fft.fft(w, N)
    A = np.fft.fft(a, N)
    return np.fft.ifft(P * np.conj(A), N).real[:n]


def _lp_weights(c, p):
    """
    Normalized weights proportional to c_k^(p-1).
    For p=1, uniform weights.  This gives a smooth approximation to the
    subgradient of the maximum coefficient.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    L = len(c)
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if p <= 1.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L)
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if cm <= 0.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L)
    cc = np.maximum(c, 1e-300)
    logw = (p - 1.0) * np.log(cc)
    logw -= float(np.max(logw))
    w = np.exp(logw)
    sw = float(np.sum(w))
    if (not np.isfinite(sw)) or sw <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L)
    return w / sw


def _soft_adam(a0, deadline, max_iters=100000, p_start=2.0, p_end=128.0,
               lr=0.05, seed=0):
    """
    Log-softmax Adam-like iteration minimizing a smoothed high-moment of conv(a,a).
    Returns (best_a, best_score).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=float).copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = len(a)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
        S = float(n)
    a /= S

    best_a = a.copy()
    c0 = _convolve_fft(a)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c0))

    x = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
    x -= float(np.mean(x))
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    beta1, beta2, eps = 0.9, 0.999, 1e-8
    t = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no_improve = 0

    while t < int(max_iters) and time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.75:
        t += 1
        frac = min(1.0, float(t) / float(max(1, max_iters)))

        a = _softmax(x)
        L = 2 * n - 1
        N = _next_pow2(L)
        A = np.fft.fft(a, N)
        c = np.fft.ifft(A * A, N).real[:L]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        score = float(2.0 * n * M)

        if score < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = score
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 2500:
            noise = 0.025 + 0.05 * (1.0 - frac)
            x += noise * rng.standard_normal(n)
            x = np.clip(x, -40.0, 40.0)
            m *= 0.5
            v *= 0.5
            no_improve = 0

        p = float(p_start + (float(p_end) - float(p_start)) * (frac * frac))
        w = _lp_weights(c, p)

        P = np.fft.fft(w, N)
        g_base = np.fft.ifft(P * np.conj(A), N).real[:n]
        g_base = 2.0 * g_base

        ga_dot = float(np.dot(a, g_base))
        gx = a * (g_base - ga_dot)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)):
            gx = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

        m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * gx
        v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * gx * gx
        m_hat = m / (1.0 - beta1 ** t)
        v_hat = v / (1.0 - beta2 ** t)
        x -= lr * m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat) + eps)
        x = np.clip(np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0), -40.0, 40.0)

    return best_a, best_score


def _lbfgs_callback(xk, fval, gnorm, normstep, *args):
    """Callback for scipy L-BFGS-B: stop when local deadline passed."""
    if time.monotonic() > _LBFSGS_DEADLINE:
        return True
    return False


def _logsumexp_objective_from_a(a, gamma):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    c = _convolve_fft(a)
    u = gamma * c
    shift = float(np.max(u))
    z = float(np.sum(np.exp(u - shift)))
    return float(shift + np.log(z))


def _logsumexp_gradient_from_a(a, gamma):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = len(a)
    c = _convolve_fft(a)
    u = gamma * c
    shift = float(np.max(u))
    e = np.exp(u - shift)
    sw = float(np.sum(e))
    if sw <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    w = e / sw
    ga = 2.0 * gamma * _corr_p_a(w, a)
    g = a * (ga - float(np.dot(a, ga)))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    return g


def _full_obj(x, gamma):
    a = _softmax(x)
    return _logsumexp_objective_from_a(a, gamma)


def _full_jac(x, gamma):
    a = _softmax(x)
    return _logsumexp_gradient_from_a(a, gamma)


def _lbfgs_full(a0, gamma, deadline, maxiter=200):
    """Full-dimensional L-BFGS on logsumexp surrogate in log-domain."""
    a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=float).copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(len(a), dtype=float)
        S = float(max(1, len(a)))
    a /= S
    n = len(a)

    best_a = a.copy()
    c0 = _convolve_fft(a)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c0))

    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
        return best_a, best_score

    x = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
    x -= float(np.mean(x))

    global _LBFSGS_DEADLINE
    _LBFSGS_DEADLINE = max(time.monotonic(), deadline - 2.0)

    try:
        res = minimize(
            _full_obj,
            x,
            args=(float(gamma),),
            jac=_full_jac,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            callback=_lbfgs_callback,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxfun": 20000,
                "maxls": 30,
                "ftol": 1e-10,
                "gtol": 1e-6,
                "disp": 0,
            },
        )
        if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            x2 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(x2)):
                a2 = _softmax(x2)
                c2 = _convolve_fft(a2)
                sc2 = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c2))
                if sc2 < best_score:
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    best_score = sc2
    except Exception:
        pass

    return best_a, best_score


def _lowdim_obj(b, F, gamma):
    a = _softmax(F @ b)
    return _logsumexp_objective_from_a(a, gamma)


def _lowdim_jac(b, F, gamma):
    a = _softmax(F @ b)
    ga = _logsumexp_gradient_from_a(a, gamma)
    gx = F.T @ ga
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)):
        return np.zeros_like(b)
    return gx


def _lowdim_start(F, b0, gamma, deadline, maxiter=80):
    """Optimize a low-dimensional feature model F @ b via logsumexp."""
    F = np.asarray(F, dtype=float)
    b0 = np.asarray(b0, dtype=float).copy()
    if F.ndim != 2 or F.shape[0] == 0:
        a = np.ones(max(1, F.shape[0]), dtype=float)
        a /= a.sum()
        c = _convolve_fft(a)
        return a, float(2.0 * len(a) * np.max(c))

    a0 = _softmax(F @ b0)
    c0 = _convolve_fft(a0)
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_score = float(2.0 * len(a0) * np.max(c0))

    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
        return best_a, best_score

    global _LBFSGS_DEADLINE
    _LBFSGS_DEADLINE = max(time.monotonic(), deadline - 2.0)

    try:
        res = minimize(
            _lowdim_obj,
            b0,
            args=(F, float(gamma)),
            jac=_lowdim_jac,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            callback=_lbfgs_callback,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxfun": 12000,
                "maxls": 30,
                "ftol": 1e-10,
                "gtol": 1e-6,
                "disp": 0,
            },
        )
        if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            b2 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(b2)):
                a2 = _softmax(F @ b2)
                c2 = _convolve_fft(a2)
                sc2 = float(2.0 * len(a2) * np.max(c2))
                if sc2 < best_score:
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    best_score = sc2
    except Exception:
        pass

    return best_a, best_score


def _feature_matrix(n, kind):
    """Smooth feature basis for log-linear nonnegative sequences."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(n)
    cols = [np.ones(n, dtype=float)]
    for p in range(1, 9):
        cols.append(t ** p)
    if kind == 0:
        cols.append(np.tanh(4.0 * (t - 0.5)))
        cols.append(np.tanh(8.0 * (t - 0.5)))
    elif kind == 1:
        for m in (1, 2, 3):
            w = 2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * t
            cols.append(np.sin(w))
            cols.append(np.cos(w))
    else:
        for m in (2, 4, 6):
            w = 2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * t
            cols.append(np.sin(w))
            cols.append(np.cos(w))
        cols.append(np.tanh(12.0 * (t - 0.5)))
    return np.column_stack(cols).astype(float)


def _ramp_start(n, rho, reverse=True):
    i = np.arange(1.0, float(n) + 1.0)
    a = i ** float(rho)
    if reverse:
        a = a[::-1].copy()
    return a


def _block_start(n, pattern):
    pat = np.asarray(pattern, dtype=float)
    m = max(1, len(pat))
    block = (np.arange(n, dtype=int) * m) // n
    return np.take(pat, block).astype(float)


def _spectral_start(n, rng, shift=0):
    """
    Start inspired by flat circular spectrum: inverse FFT of symmetric +/-1
    signs, then take a positive one-sided projection.
    """
    N = _next_pow2(2 * n)
    s = rng.choice(np.array([-1.0, 1.0]), size=N)
    s[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, N // 2 + 1):
        s[N - k] = s[k]
    if N % 2 == 0:
        s[N // 2] = 1.0
    v = np.fft.ifft(s).real
    amax = float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
    if amax > 1e-12:
        v /= amax
    if shift:
        v = np.roll(v, int(shift))
    arr = v[:n]
    rn = float(arr.max() - arr.min())
    offset = -float(arr.min()) + 0.05 * max(1e-12, rn)
    a = np.maximum(arr + offset, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float) / float(n)
    return a / S


def _make_candidate(a, idx, group):
    """Validate and normalize a candidate; return sorted tuple or None."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size < 2:
        return None
    if a.size > 4096:
        a = a[:4096]
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return None
    a = a / S
    c = _convolve_fft(a)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    score = float(2.0 * len(a) * M)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return None
    return (score, int(idx), int(len(a)), str(group), a)


def _newton_flatten(a0, deadline, max_steps=30):
    """
    Active-set Newton-style refinement.
    For the largest convolution lags, solve weighted linearized least squares
    for a delta that equalizes those lags, then line-search on actual score.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=float).copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(len(a), dtype=float)
        S = float(max(1, len(a)))
    a /= S
    n = len(a)

    c0 = _convolve_fft(a)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c0))
    best_a = a.copy()

    if n < 4 or time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
        return best_a, best_score

    max_r = min(n // 2, 256) if n <= 700 else 0
    r_list = []
    if max_r >= 8:
        for rr in (8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256):
            if rr <= max_r and rr not in r_list:
                r_list.append(rr)
        if max_r not in r_list:
            r_list.append(max_r)
        r_list.sort()

    for r in r_list:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        fails = 0
        for _ in range(int(max_steps)):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
                break
            if fails > 6:
                break

            c = _convolve_fft(a)
            cur_score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
            if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = cur_score
                best_a = a.copy()

            if r <= 1:
                break
            active_idx = np.argsort(c)[-r:][::-1]
            active_c = c[active_idx]
            target = float(np.mean(active_c))
            max_active = float(active_c[0])
            if target >= max_active * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                break

            K = np.zeros((r, n), dtype=float)
            for row in range(r):
                m = int(active_idx[row])
                lo = max(0, m - n + 1)
                hi = min(n - 1, m)
                if hi >= lo:
                    idxs = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=int)
                    K[row, idxs] = a[m - idxs]

            wp = 0.7 ** np.arange(r, dtype=float)
            wp = wp / max(1e-300, float(np.sum(wp)))
            sqrtw = np.sqrt(wp)
            rhs = 0.5 * (target - active_c) * sqrtw
            Ks = K * sqrtw[:, None]

            try:
                delta, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(Ks, rhs, rcond=1e-10)
            except Exception:
                break

            delta = np.asarray(delta, dtype=float)
            delta -= float(np.mean(delta))
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(delta)):
                break
            dn = float(np.linalg.norm(delta))
            if dn <= 1e-14:
                break
            cap = max(1e-6, 2.0 * float(np.linalg.norm(a)))
            if dn > cap:
                delta *= cap / dn

            accepted = False
            for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
                if accepted:
                    break
                for eps in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03, 0.01):
                    an = a + sign * eps * delta
                    an = np.maximum(an, 0.0)
                    Snew = float(np.sum(an))
                    if Snew <= 1e-12:
                        continue
                    an = an / Snew
                    cn = _convolve_fft(an)
                    sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(cn))
                    if sc < best_score - 1e-13:
                        a = an
                        best_a = an.copy()
                        best_score = sc
                        accepted = True
                        fails = 0
                        break
            if not accepted:
                fails += 1

    return best_a, best_score


def _refine_candidate(a0, deadline, budget, seed=0):
    """Refine one candidate, interleaving soft and polynomial/Newton polishers."""
    a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=float).copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = len(a)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return None, float("inf")
    a /= S

    c0 = _convolve_fft(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c0))

    budget = float(budget)
    if budget <= 5.0:
        return best_a, best_score

    start = time.monotonic()
    stop_all = min(deadline, start + budget)

    if stop_all - time.monotonic() > 20.0:
        if budget >= 120.0:
            stop_low = min(stop_all, start + 0.22 * budget)
            if stop_low - time.monotonic() > 5.0:
                a1, s1 = _soft_adam(
                    best_a, stop_low, max_iters=200000,
                    p_start=2.0, p_end=32.0, lr=0.06, seed=seed + 1
                )
                if s1 < best_score - 1e-13:
                    best_a, best_score = a1, s1

            if n <= 4096 and (stop_all - time.monotonic()) > 60.0:
                alloc = min(50.0, 0.35 * (stop_all - time.monotonic()))
                if time.monotonic() + alloc < stop_all - 5.0:
                    a2, s2 = _lbfgs_full(best_a, 10.0 * float(n),
                                         time.monotonic() + alloc, maxiter=150)
                    if s2 < best_score - 1e-13:
                        best_a, best_score = a2, s2

            if n <= 700 and (stop_all - time.monotonic()) > 60.0:
                alloc = min(55.0, 0.35 * (stop_all - time.monotonic()))
                if time.monotonic() + alloc < stop_all - 5.0:
                    a3, s3 = _newton_flatten(best_a, time.monotonic() + alloc,
                                             max_steps=35)
                    if s3 < best_score - 1e-13:
                        best_a, best_score = a3, s3

            stop_mid = min(stop_all, start + 0.72 * budget)
            if stop_mid - time.monotonic() > 10.0:
                a4, s4 = _soft_adam(
                    best_a, stop_mid, max_iters=300000,
                    p_start=8.0, p_end=128.0, lr=0.022, seed=seed + 2
                )
                if s4 < best_score - 1e-13:
                    best_a, best_score = a4, s4

            if n <= 4096 and (stop_all - time.monotonic()) > 40.0:
                alloc = min(55.0, 0.45 * (stop_all - time.monotonic()))
                if time.monotonic() + alloc < stop_all - 5.0:
                    a5, s5 = _lbfgs_full(best_a, 40.0 * float(n),
                                         time.monotonic() + alloc, maxiter=180)
                    if s5 < best_score - 1e-13:
                        best_a, best_score = a5, s5

            if n <= 700 and (stop_all - time.monotonic()) > 35.0:
                alloc = min(50.0, 0.45 * (stop_all - time.monotonic()))
                if time.monotonic() + alloc < stop_all - 5.0:
                    a6, s6 = _newton_flatten(best_a, time.monotonic() + alloc,
                                             max_steps=30)
                    if s6 < best_score - 1e-13:
                        best_a, best_score = a6, s6

            if stop_all - time.monotonic() > 15.0:
                a7, s7 = _soft_adam(
                    best_a, stop_all, max_iters=300000,
                    p_start=32.0, p_end=256.0, lr=0.008, seed=seed + 3
                )
                if s7 < best_score - 1e-13:
                    best_a, best_score = a7, s7
        else:
            a1, s1 = _soft_adam(
                best_a, stop_all, max_iters=300000,
                p_start=4.0, p_end=128.0, lr=0.035, seed=seed + 1
            )
            if s1 < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_a, best_score = a1, s1

    return best_a, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380.0, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for the best non-negative sequence within the time budget.
    Returns a Python list of floats.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        bfloat = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        bfloat = 2380.0
    if not np.isfinite(bfloat) or bfloat <= 0.0:
        bfloat = 2380.0
    bfloat = min(bfloat, 2390.0)
    deadline = t_start + max(10.0, bfloat - 8.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if np.isfinite(float(seed)) else 42)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    cands = []
    idx = 0

    # ---------------- Candidate generation: previous best ----------------
    try:
        hs = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)
        if hs.ndim == 1:
            hs = np.where(np.isfinite(hs), hs, 0.0)
            if hs.size > 2048:
                hs = hs[:2048]
            if hs.size >= 2:
                r1 = _make_candidate(hs, idx, "prev")
                if r1 is not None:
                    cands.append(r1)
                    idx += 1
                r2 = _make_candidate(hs[::-1], idx, "prev_rev")
                if r2 is not None:
                    cands.append(r2)
                    idx += 1
                r3 = _make_candidate(0.98 * hs + 0.02 * rng.random(hs.size),
                                     idx, "prev_perturb")
                if r3 is not None:
                    cands.append(r3)
                    idx += 1
    except Exception:
        pass

    # ---------------- Low-dimensional optimized starts ----------------
    remaining0 = deadline - t_start
    lowdim_budget = 0.0
    if remaining0 > 300.0:
        lowdim_budget = min(180.0, max(30.0, 0.07 * remaining0))
    lowdim_stop = t_start + lowdim_budget

    if lowdim_budget > 45.0:
        for nn in (512, 256, 1024):
            for kind in (0, 1):
                if time.monotonic() > lowdim_stop:
                    break
                F = _feature_matrix(nn, kind)
                d = int(F.shape[1])
                group = "lowdim_%d_%d" % (nn, kind)

                b0 = np.zeros(d, dtype=float)
                if time.monotonic() < lowdim_stop - 5.0:
                    aa, _ = _lowdim_start(F, b0, 8.0 * float(nn),
                                          lowdim_stop, maxiter=100)
                    cc = _make_candidate(aa, idx, group)
                    if cc is not None:
                        cands.append(cc)
                        idx += 1

                b1 = rng.normal(0.0, 0.6, size=d)
                if time.monotonic() < lowdim_stop - 5.0:
                    aa, _ = _lowdim_start(F, b1, 14.0 * float(nn),
                                          lowdim_stop, maxiter=70)
                    cc = _make_candidate(aa, idx, group + "_rnd")
                    if cc is not None:
                        cands.append(cc)
                        idx += 1

                b2 = np.arange(d, dtype=float) * 0.18
                if time.monotonic() < lowdim_stop - 5.0:
                    aa, _ = _lowdim_start(F, b2, 18.0 * float(nn),
                                          lowdim_stop, maxiter=60)
                    cc = _make_candidate(aa, idx, group + "_poly")
                    if cc is not None:
                        cands.append(cc)
                        idx += 1

    # ---------------- Deterministic / random starts ----------------
    start_list = (128, 256, 512, 1024)
    for nn in start_list:
        base = nn
        # Uniform
        r = _make_candidate(np.ones(base), idx, "uni%d" % base)
        if r is not None:
            cands.append(r); idx += 1

        # Power ramps and reversed ramps
        for rho in (1.0, 1.6, 2.2, 3.0):
            r = _make_candidate(_ramp_start(base, rho, reverse=True),
                                idx, "ramp%d_rho%.1f" % (base, rho))
            if r is not None:
                cands.append(r); idx += 1
            r = _make_candidate(_ramp_start(base, rho, reverse=False),
                                idx, "ramp%d_rho%.1f_nv" % (base, rho))
            if r is not None:
                cands.append(r); idx += 1

        # Exponential ramps
        t = (np.arange(base, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(base)
        for rho in (2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
            a = np.exp(rho * t)
            r = _make_candidate(a, idx, "exp%d_rho%.0f" % (base, rho))
            if r is not None:
                cands.append(r); idx += 1
            r = _make_candidate(a[::-1], idx, "exp%d_rho%.0f_rev" % (base, rho))
            if r is not None:
                cands.append(r); idx += 1

        # Blocks
        patterns = (
            [1.0, 2.0],
            [0.6, 1.0, 1.8],
            [1.0, 0.45, 1.0, 0.45],
            [1.0, 1.0, 1.8, 1.0, 0.55],
            [1.0, 1.5, 0.55, 1.5],
            [0.8, 1.2, 1.6, 1.2, 0.8],
        )
        for pi, pat in enumerate(patterns):
            r = _make_candidate(_block_start(base, pat),
                                idx, "block%d_%d" % (base, pi))
            if r is not None:
                cands.append(r); idx += 1

        # Cosines / chirps
        i = np.arange(base, dtype=float)
        for fr in (1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0, 8.0):
            a = 0.55 + 0.45 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * fr * i / float(base))
            r = _make_candidate(a, idx, "cos%d_f%.0f" % (base, fr))
            if r is not None:
                cands.append(r); idx += 1

        # Random Dirichlet
        for alpha in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0):
            a = rng.dirichlet(np.full(base, float(alpha)))
            r = _make_candidate(a, idx, "dir%d_a%.1f" % (base, alpha))
            if r is not None:
                cands.append(r); idx += 1

        # Flat-spectrum-inspired starts
        for shift in (0, 2, 7, 16):
            a = _spectral_start(base, rng, shift)
            r = _make_candidate(a, idx, "frame%d_s%d" % (base, shift))
            if r is not None:
                cands.append(r); idx += 1

    # ---------------- Select diverse, promising candidates ----------------
    if cands:
        cands.sort()
        selected = []
        count_by_n = {}
        for cand in cands:
            if len(selected) >= 14:
                break
            nn = int(cand[2])
            if nn > 2048:
                continue
            if count_by_n.get(nn, 0) >= 4:
                continue
            selected.append(cand)
            count_by_n[nn] = count_by_n.get(nn, 0) + 1
    else:
        selected = []

    # ---------------- Refine selected candidates ----------------
    if selected:
        for rank, cand in enumerate(selected):
            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if remaining < 90.0:
                break
            if rank == 0:
                alloc = min(0.38 * remaining, 450.0)
            elif rank < 3:
                alloc = min(0.24 * remaining, 300.0)
            else:
                alloc = min(0.14 * remaining, 150.0)
            alloc = max(35.0, min(alloc, max(5.0, remaining - 20.0)))

            a0 = cand[4]
            a1, s1 = _refine_candidate(a0, deadline, alloc, seed=int(seed) + 100 + rank)
            if np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_score:
                best_score = float(s1)
                best_seq = np.asarray(a1, dtype=float).copy()
                print("  refined candidate n=%d rank=%d score=%.7f" %
                      (len(a1), rank, best_score), flush=True)

    # ---------------- Fallback if no valid candidate was found ----------------
    if best_seq is None:
        fb = np.ones(256, dtype=float)
        fb /= float(fb.sum())
        cf = _convolve_fft(fb)
        best_score = float(2.0 * 256 * np.max(cf))
        best_seq = fb
        print("  fallback to uniform 256, score=%.7f" % best_score, flush=True)

    # ---------------- Final intensive refinement of global best ----------------
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 90.0:
        alloc = min(remaining - 5.0, max(90.0, 0.42 * remaining))
        a2, s2 = _refine_candidate(best_seq, deadline, alloc,
                                   seed=int(seed) + 1000)
        if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
            best_score = float(s2)
            best_seq = np.asarray(a2, dtype=float).copy()
            print("  final refinement score=%.7f" % best_score, flush=True)

    # ---------------- Sanitize and return ----------------
    best_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=float)
    best_seq = np.where(np.isfinite(best_seq), best_seq, 0.0)
    best_seq = np.clip(best_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(np.sum(best_seq))
    if S <= 1e-12:
        best_seq = np.ones(256, dtype=float) / 256.0
    else:
        best_seq = best_seq / S

    seq_list = [float(x) for x in best_seq]
    try:
        ev = float(evaluate_sequence(seq_list))
        print("  final evaluate_sequence score=%.7f, n=%d" % (ev, len(seq_list)),
              flush=True)
    except Exception as e:
        print("  evaluate_sequence error: %s" % e, flush=True)

    return seq_list
```
